## Bank reader controls

These rows concern parsing, allocation and ownership. ADP-1 does not exempt any of them. Result numbers are the native integers; symbolic Wwise result names are not inferred.

| Step | Address | Behaviour | Gates | Order / failure results | Float bits / uncertainty |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| K1 | 0x009B21F4..0x009B2260 | Read eight-byte chunk header, require BKHD, then read the twenty-byte fixed header. | The first read must return 1; tag must match. | First-read failure and wrong tag return 7. The fixed-header read propagates its result. | No DSP. |
| K2 | 0x009B2264..0x009B22AC | When global key 0x0108D9A0 is nonzero, XOR the first sixteen bytes of the fixed header with that word. Leave the final word unchanged. | Nonzero key only. | Happens before header-size/version checks. | Key-writer census remains UNKNOWN, recoverable; no claim that the key is always zero. |
| K3 | 0x009B22B0..0x009B230C | Skip declared header size minus twenty; require actual skipped count to match. Require version 0x78. Store parser+64 from `(header halfword +0C != 0)`. | Nonzero excess size calls skip; version check follows successful skip. | Skip error propagates; short skip returns 0x38; wrong version returns 0x40; success 1. The parser flag store also occurs on error exits. | Do not replace the actual-count comparison with unconditional failure for an extended header. |
| K4 | 0x009B74D8..0x009B7688 | Find an existing bank under the global bank mutex. An existing bank whose +50 bit1 is set is rejected; otherwise publish it and optionally increment +54. New-bank path allocates 0x58 and initializes fields before opening its reader. | Existing key includes the mode-2 memory-window identity. | Existing pending bank returns 0x45. Allocation failure returns 0x34 after reader reset/close. | The allocator and reader results remain native inputs. |
| K5 | 0x009B768C..0x009B78BC | Open the selected reader, require BKHD success, then repeatedly read eight-byte chunk headers. Dispatch STMG, ENVS, PLAT, INIT, DIDX, HIRC, STID and DATA by the exact four-byte tag. | Actual count 0 ends the bank; counts 1..7 are invalid. | Reader error propagates; short header returns 7. Each selected chunk handler must return 1 before the next header. | Tags are raw little-endian words, not case-folded strings. |
| K6 | 0x009B78C0..0x009B78E8 | Unknown chunk: skip its declared byte count and compare the actual count. | Used for unknown tags and selected skip modes. | Mismatched actual count returns 7. The skip helper's return value is not tested in this slice. | Preserve the distinction between return status and actual count. |
| K7 | 0x009B78EC..0x009B79DC | DIDX count is integer floor(size/12); read or borrow exactly count*12 bytes, set the media-table ownership bit for allocated storage, and publish count when the pointer is nonnull. | Table modes 0/1/3 process it; mode2 skips. Existing +2C uses skip. | Null allocation returns 0x34. The allocated DIDX read result is not checked here. | No divisibility validation or trailing-byte skip is invented. Modes above 3 take the next-header branch without consuming the payload in this slice. |
| K8 | 0x009B79E0..0x009B7B08 | DATA mode0 borrows the memory-window payload for input mode2 or obtains a pool buffer; stores byte size and reads the payload. With no +2C table and nonzero +30 count, call 0x009B49A4. | Table modes1/3 use 0x009B6C34; mode2 skips; larger modes resume header loop. | Handler errors propagate. Zero DATA size uses the existing pointer. | Media-table population callee is a separate KEEP obligation. |

## RTPC curve values and accumulation (M6-009; C35)

Notation: every arithmetic operator below is a separate binary32 operation in instruction order. VMLA/VMLS are the shipped VFP non-fused multiply-accumulate/subtract operations; do not algebraically reassociate. `F(bits)` means reinterpret the stated32bit word asbinary32; `B(f)` reinterprets back. The instruction companion `20261007-sound-rtpc-native.txt` includes each operation and literal address. This custom curve arithmetic produces control values and is fully outside the ADP-1 sample-DSP exception.

| Step | Address | Behaviour | Gates | Order / failure | Float bits |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| R1 | 0x00A14E28..0x00A14E94 | Curve points are12bytes `(x,y,shapeu32)`. Starts atcallerhint; if hint>=count-1 returns lasty, otherwise advances until x0>=input or input<x1. | Unsigned hint bound; VCMPE/BGE atA14E58 and BMI atA14E70 preserve unordered comparison behavior. | No backwards search, no empty-array guard. | Endpoint data words used asbinary32. |
| R2 | 0x00A14EC0..0x00A14ED0;0x00A14F30..0x00A14F54;0x00A15078..0x00A15090 | x0>=input or shape9 yieldslefty; shape4 computes t=(input-x0)/(x1-x0), y=lefty+t*(righty-lefty). Others normalize t beforedispatch. | Shape stored asinteger, not subnormal float (decompiler types are misleading). | No divide-by-zero protection; hint written after evaluation before scaling. | Binary32; shape4 uses VMLA.F32, not hostdouble. |
| R3 | 0x00A14F58..0x00A14F84;0x00A151FC..0x00A15244 | Shape0..8 dispatchjump table;9step. Unsupported shape writes hint then returns scaling-dependent fallback. | Unsigned shape<=8, exact table order0,1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8. | Unsupported: scaling3 evaluatespow at0; scaling4 returns3F7FC105; scaling2 fastlogat0; other returns00000000. | Fallback bits exact. |
| R4 | 0x00A150E4..0x00A15100;0x00A151E8..0x00A151F8 | Shape0: u=1-t, u2=u*u,u3=u*u2,delta=left-right,y=right+u3*delta. Shape8: t2=t*t,t3=t*t2,delta=right-left,y=left+t3*delta. | Shape selector. | Operations as written. | One3F800000. |
| R5 | 0x00A1513C..0x00A15158;0x00A15190..0x00A151AC | Shape2: u=t*(t-3);u*=half;delta=left-right;y=left+u*delta. Shape6: u=t*(t+1);u*=half;delta=right-left;y=left+u*delta. | Shape selector. | Preserve order and delta sign. | Three40400000,half3F000000,one3F800000. |
| R6 | 0x00A15104..0x00A15138 | Shape1: u=t*3FC90FDB,q=u*u; a=3C081741+q*B9408E8F; b=3E2AA5D9-q*a; c=3F7FFFC7+q*b; u*=c; y=left+u*(right-left). | Shape1. | VMLA then VNMLS then VMLA; no phone sine call. | All named coefficients literal bits. |

Other branch (including unordered) selects u=pi-t*pi and replaces final factor with1-factor. q=u*u; a=3B881741+q*B8C08E8F; b=3DAAA5D9-q*a; c=3EFFFFC7+q*b; factor=u*c; y=left+factor*(right-left). | VCMPE/BLS selects ordered lower-or-equal branch; unordered has C1/Z0 and does not take BLS. | Endpoint branch matters at half/NaN. | Half3F000000,one3F800000; exact coefficients. |

| R10 | 0x00A14E98..0x00A14EB8;0x00A1503C..0x00A15050 | Writes segment hint then selects scale3fastpow, scale4multiplyby3D4CCCCD thenfastpow, scale2fastlog; other scales returny unchanged. | Scale3/4 y<C2140000 returns00000000 viaBMI. Scale4 tests after multiplication. | Hint store precedes all returns. | Scale4 factor3D4CCCCD, cutoffC2140000. |

Below-1 or above1 returns corresponding saturation. z=1+sign*y; mantissa/exponent split; q=(mant-1)/(mant+1); r=(exp-127)*ln2+(q+q)*(1+q*q/3); result=((r*log10e)*20)*sign. | Exact BMI/BGT branches; endpoints remain in bit-log path. | All VFP operations separate; no phone log invocation. |127=42FE0000,ln2=3F317218,third3EAAAAAB,log10e3EDE5BD9,20=41A00000. |
| R13 | 0x00A17724..0x00A17874;0x00A17878..0x00A179C8 | Product accumulator starts3F800000;sum starts00000000. Iterates0x14-byte binding records inphysicalorder. Copies six-word callerkey perbinding; callsA17280; when lookupfails uses hash-table default or0. If resolver'sskip byte0 evaluatescurve and foldswithVMUL/VADD.F32. | Empty(count*0x14==0) returnsidentity; skipbyte!=0 contributesnothing. | Resolver beforedefault fallback beforecurve beforefold; no tree/reorderedreduce. | Product/sum results arecontrolvalues, KEEP. Key mutation islocalperbinding. |

## Name hash and RNG initialization correction

The current M6-005 quotation above says the hashed input is `min(L,0x102)` bytes. The following instructions contradict that bound: the copy count is capped, but the lowercase and hash loop endpoints use the original `strlen`, held in r6. This is a finding about native behaviour, not authorization to change the manifest or production code.

| Step | Address | Behaviour | Gates | Order / failure results | Float bits / uncertainty |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| N1 | 0x0099DB84..0x0099DBE0 | Null name returns0. Nonnull computes original length L; copies min(L+1,0x103) bytes to stack+4 and writes NUL at that capped copy count. | Empty nonnull string still takes copy path. | Length retained separately from capped copy count. | No float; copy cap259 is not the later loop bound. |
| N2 | 0x0099DBE4..0x0099DC00 | Lowercase ASCII A..Z by adding0x20, iterating exactly L bytes from stack+4. | L0 skips loop. | Lowercasing precedes hashing. | For L>0x103 the loop reads/writes outside the copied region; stack contents and resulting fault/hash are runtime-dependent UNKNOWN. No truncated-name result is asserted. |
| N3 | 0x0099DC04..0x0099DC50 | Start hash811C9DC5; for L bytes compute `(hash*01000193) XOR byte` modulo32 bits. Empty string returns811C9DC5. | Uses original L, not copy count or first NUL. | Multiply then XOR: FNV-1. | Safe, supplied bytes determine normal-length hash; no Unicode/locale lowercasing. |
| N4 | 0x0099EF7C..0x0099EF88;0x009B0200..0x009B0210;0x0099DB58..0x0099DB7C | Init calls9B0200, which calls SetSeed with both words0. SetSeed's zero test invokes phone time(NULL), sign-extends its32-bit return, and stores both words to shared LCG state. Nonzero64-bit seed stores unchanged. | Both words zero, not just low word zero. | Seeding occurs before9B0200 posts tag36 at9B022C. | No initial draw. M6-021's live Unix-seconds conclusion holds; its cited9EF80 is an indirect caller via9B0200, not the SetSeed call instruction. |
| N5 | 0x009AE3F8..0x009AE408 | Audio message handler passes message+04 as low seed word and high word0 to SetSeed, then consumes the message. | Low word0 invokes the same time(NULL) fallback. | Seed update on message dispatch; not producer enqueue time. | No draw or normalization in this handler. Caller/tag registration remains part of the union census. |

## EQ / peak-limiter VERIFY boundaries

These entries settle the named setup spans as mixed control/coefficient work, not pure per-sample loops. The permitted DSP subset remains only the PCM arithmetic invoked after these choices. This is not a claim that the DSP equivalence test has passed.

| F2 | 0x00AA1B98..0x00AA1BD4 | Limiter Init stores parameter pointer+0C, allocator+10, sample rate+18 and packed format word+1C from format+00/+04, parameter+10 to cached+14, format+08 halfword to+20 and+0A byte to+22; tail-call setup AA19CC. | No validation gate in this wrapper. | Store format/parameters before setup. | Raw parameter word retained; no invented defaults. |

If result!=1 OR fourth argument nonzero, return saved result immediately. | Fourth argument can skip common body even after successful virtual read. | Initial virtual reader first; no common cursor consumption on early return. | Do not treat fourth argument as a failure result. |

Consume second u32 optional parent ID; nonzero resolves selector0 and uses same retain/link/release order, but missing parent continues without error. | Two missing-ID paths differ. First ID0 and second ID0 skip lookup. | First link completes before second ID consumption; parent release before link-result gate. | Child retain is not locally undone on failed link; recipient ownership must be traced. No float operations. |

Unchanged bit skips its callback. Bits2/3/4/5 map byte47bits1/2/3/4 together afterward. | Callback after first bit may alter later node flags; node byte45 is reloaded before second comparison. | First change/callback -> second change/callback -> remaining flag publication. | Boolean flag bits, no whole-byte overwrite. |

On success, advance shared cursor by count*12 and subtract that byte count from remaining-size word. | No local point decoding or extent validation before virtual call. | Header cursor publication -> subscription virtual -> payload cursor/remaining adjustment. | Point triples retain raw binary32 words for the descendant to interpret. |

## Node state-group binding creation and replacement

Native companion: `20261007-sound-state-group-reader-native.txt`.

| Step | Address | Behaviour | Gates | Order / failure results | Float bits / uncertainty |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| SG1 | 0x009F6078..0x009F6130;0x009F617C..0x009F6188 | Search node list+18 via next+8 for group ID+20; existing returns pointer without notifying node. Missing allocate2C, null returnsnull. Initialize owner+C, ID20, vt, zero4/8/10/14/18/1C/24 and byte28. Register via A27B20(manager,groupID,newBinding); non1 uses SG2 failure cleanup. | Creation flag does not force notification for existing binding. | Init before manager registration. | No whole-object memset, no group ID clamp. |
| SG2 | 0x009F6134..0x009F6178;0x009F618C..0x009F61D0 | Registration failure frees optional array14 after count18 clear, then optional array10, then binding; returnnull. Success prepends binding to node list18, queries current group value A28198 and stores raw word24, then if creation flag nonzero calls owner vt+CC; return binding pointer. | Notification occurs after list publication and current-value store. | Manager registration -> list prepend -> current-state read/store -> node notification. | A27B20/A28198 manager bodies still pending. |
| SG3 | 0x009F24C8..0x009F2574 | Pair setter resolves argument1 object ID under registry mutex+28, bucket pointer2C/count30; found increments object refC before unlocking. Missing unlocks without object. Search group array14/count18, stride12, for argument2 key (bank pair's first word). Missing object AND absent key returns10. | Bucket count0 bypasses division. | Resolve/retain object before looking at existing binding. | No numeric conversion; raw IDs, not pointer hash. |
| SG4 | 0x009F2578..0x009F2730 | Existing key: if stored object+4 equals newly retained object, release extra retain if nonnull and return1; stored object-ID word+8 is not rewritten on that shortcut. If objects differ, call A270B0(old), release old vt+C, re-search array because collaborator may change it, remove matching entry by stable memmove/count--. Missing new object then returns10. Nonnull new object replaces/inserts key/object/rawID entry. | If collaborator already removed entry, skip removal and continue to insertion. | Old detach -> old release -> re-search/remove -> new publication. | No rollback on inability to insert replacement. |
| SG5 | 0x009F25D0..0x009F2634;0x009F26FC..0x009F272C;0x009F2754..0x009F2804 | New/replacement array grows capacity1C by1, allocate capacity*12, copy live triples/free old/publish. ## M9-007 curve-to-parameter cross-check

> M9-007 — title: “The note-off envelope binding applies Wwise scaling 2 after its curve”; status: IMPLEMENTATION_GAP; evidence: “0x00A14F88..0x00A15038”, “re-analysis/inventory/M6-wwise-bank.md”, “Cozmo.bnk object 381606890 and binding 462443456”; unresolved: “the note-off envelope runs from the note-on for the whole note, but with property 15 = 2 the gate refuses it for a note-on; scaling 2 uses Math.Log10 where the engine uses its fast log (0x00A14FD8..0x00A15034); the test is circular.”

Reopened companions `20261007-sound-envelope-scaling2-native.txt` and `20261007-sound-envelope-curve-control-native.txt` agree with R1–R12/CL1–CL3 for the supplied scalar curve and scaling2 path. A14F88 is the **scaling2 branch**; MT7 is the native MIDI event predicate. These scalar values are RTPC/control outputs and remain exact under ADP-1. No host Log10 replacement is justified. The class-specific envelope trigger/timing and its complete live recipients are still unread; this cross-check does not complete the production claim or fix the cited circular test.

## Shipped libc++ prime-size collaborator

Primary companions: `20261007-libcxx-prime-native.py`, `20261007-sound-libcxx-prime-native.txt`; library `resources/lib/armeabi-v7a/libc++_shared.so`, SHA256 `8ac5090bbd0be7401af5fff6044ded6f1e6ced692fcd3581fa3cb937b519a16a`. Addresses in this subsection are **library VAs**, not engine VAs. This dependency closes UC21's prime-size algorithm, without extending into unrelated standard-library functions.

| Step | Address | Behaviour | Gates | Order / failure results | Float bits / uncertainty |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| UC24 | libc++ 0x0003B038..0x0003B072;table0009B480 (48 u32 words in companion) | For unsigned input<=D3, lower_bound over all48 small-prime entries returns first>=input. Table begins0,2,3,5,7; endsC5,C7,D3. Input0 returns0,input1 returns2. Binary search advances base only when midpoint value<input. | No linear scan or randomized primality decision. | Small-input branch before overflow gate. | Integer table, every raw word captured. |
| UC25 | libc++ 0x0003B074..0x0003B618;table0009B540 (48 u32 words);division0008675C..000867F4 | Input>=FFFFFFFC throws (UC26). Otherwise quotient=UMUL64(input>>1,9C09C09D)>>38; base=wrap32(quotient*D2), residue=input−base. Lower_bound in48 wheel210 residues chooses starting candidate base+residueTable[index]. Candidate tests small-prime indices5..46 (11..199) in order: q=unsigned candidate/divisor; q<divisor accepts candidate; q*divisor==candidate rejects. Then test wheel divisors211,221,223,...419,421,... via the native unrolled offsets (same wheel table shifted by210 blocks). Each test uses same quotient-square stop/divisibility gate. Reject advances candidate residue index, wraps48→0 and increments base quotient; accept returns candidate. | Division by positive divisors only; division helper supplies exact unsigned quotient through shift/subtract or power-of-two shift. Overflow guard precedes candidate walk. | Deterministic ascending candidate and divisor order; no RNG/state. | No float intermediates. Full wheel values and native offsets in companion, including composite trial divisors. |
| UC26 | libc++ 0x0003B622..0x0003B656;string0003B66C;relocations000A4D1C/20/24 | Overflow branch constructs exception with text `__next_prime overflow`, installs overflow_error vtable, throws with typeinfo overflow_error and destructorD1 (resolved relocation names/values in companion). Constructor/throw failures propagate; landing pad frees exception then resumes unwind. | Unsigned input>=FFFFFFFC. Largest representable primeFFFFFFFB is accepted input. | Overflow before wheel search; no wrapped candidate success. | Exception-runtime mechanics are outside this bounded helper; exception choice/message and branch exact. |

## Sink conversion selection and sample-arithmetic boundary

Primary companion: `20261007-sound-sink-conversion-boundary-native.txt`. Input and destination are descriptors, with buffer at0, packed format at4, capacity u16 atC, filled u16 atE. K is the converter whose u16 at0 is the requested count and binary32 at4 is the ramp reciprocal. This boundary adds no permission to change channel selection, counts, output layout or silence placement. The arithmetic-only portions remain subject to the ADP-1 emulator equivalence test, not settled by this extraction.

Selection is based on the input mask, without normalizing or validating channel count. | Exact mask equality, not popcount. | Format extraction →ordered branches →selected kernel. | No conversion status is returned or tested by DF49. Branches/counts/layout are KEEP. |
| CV2 | 0x00A46164..0x00A461D0;0x00A463B4..0x00A463DC | 63F/60F and mask3-special paths pass K,input descriptor,destination buffer,start gain raw bits,and binary32 delta=K4*(end−start). Mask63F→A46B50,60F→A469B0,mask3-special→A467B8. No descriptor or K state publication in these dispatch blocks. | Selected only by CV1/CV3. | Gain endpoints →delta →call →return. | Exact endpoint values and kernel selection; per-sample gain arithmetic is the equivalent boundary. |

Other input masks call A44E30(input buffer,destination buffer,K.count,destination format,start,end). No result gate. | Destination mask comparison ignores the low12 bits; generic kernel still receives full destination format. | Source/destination layout decision →raw buffers/count/formats/endpoints →call. | Raw channel ordering belongs to KEEP, not an arbitrary downmix. Generic mapping detailed in CV6/CV7. |
| CV4 | 0x00A46278..0x00A462D4;0x00A46320..0x00A4632C;0x00A463A4..0x00A463B0 | Input mask4 writes planar float destination. Channel byte=input format low8; source plane stride=input capacityC*4, destination plane stride=destination capacityC*4. Each plane's end is start+K.count*4. No zero-count pre-test before vector loop; count0 still executes its first8-float block. | Plane base →gain branch →sample block loop →next channel. | Structural post-tests/plane layout exact. PCM multiplication/ramp association and NEON lane arithmetic at A462D8..A4631C/A46330..A463A0 may be equivalent; no other state/recipient in those bodies. |

Each output frame uses the corresponding same source frame index. Output increments10/60/80 hex bytes respectively. | Partial block counts truncate; don't round up or fabricate remainder processing. | Plane pointers →gain lanes →block loop →return. | Arithmetic/gain SIMD association is eligible under ADP-1; channel interleave and block coverage remain exact.
